Refuses to fabricate candidates. ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memo/commands/memo-perspective.md) · ★1)

- **memo-render**: PDF renderer for the memo skill. Reads the latest &lt;thread&gt;.md and produces &lt;thread&gt;.pdf via the pandoc + weasyprint/wkhtmltopdf/xelatex chain, recording render-gate findings in _progress.json. Optional, non-blocking, idempotent. ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memo/commands/memo-render.md) · ★1)
- **memo-revise**: Reviser command for the memo skill. Reads the latest version + all critic siblings and produces the next version with a changelog mapping critic notes to revisions. ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memo/commands/memo-revise.md) · ★1)

- **memoir**: Per-chapter-thread status orchestrator for the memoir skill. Discovers memoir threads under cwd, reports state-machine position per thread (including corpus/voice tier declaration status), and recommends the next command. Read-only. Does NOT rebuild anvil:project-book's portfolio view — use /anvil:project-book for the all-chapters table. ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memoir/commands/memoir.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **memoir-audit**: Auditor for the memoir skill. Always writes a general factual/narrative-consistency sibling. When the corpus tier (#597) is active, ALSO writes the exhaustive kind:tool_evidence corpus-audit critic per anvil/lib/snippets/provenance.md §Section 4 as a &lt;thread&gt;.{N}.corpus-audit/ sibling — running the anchor-drift pre-pass (§Section 4a, ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memoir/commands/memoir-audit.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **memoir-draft**: Drafter for the memoir skill. Produces the first version of a chapter (LaTeX body) from the project BRIEF, the resolved dual-corpus (#597) and dual-voice (#598) tiers, and any thread refs, writing a provenance.md claim-to-source map before prose. EMPTY → DRAFTED transition. ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memoir/commands/memoir-draft.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **memoir-figures**: Figurer for the memoir skill. Renders diagrams (mmdc → PNG) and resolves the memoir-local photo-placement macros (\famphoto/\fullphoto/\marginphoto) against anvil:project-photos' manifest.json, plus an optional PDF from the LaTeX source, reusing anvil/lib/render.py + anvil/lib/render_gate.py. No new rendering pipeline. Runs any time after draft/revise (no AUDITED gate). ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memoir/commands/memoir-figures.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **memoir-review**: Reviewer for the memoir skill. Scores the 9-dimension /44 anvil-memoir-v1 rubric (≥39 advance) with sourcing fidelity as owned dominant dim 1, spot-samples the provenance.md back-check (#597), and scores narrator + subject voice fidelity (#598) when active. Runs parallel with memoir-audit. DRAFTED/REVISED → REVIEWED transition. ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memoir/commands/memoir-review.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **memoir-revise**: Reviser for the memoir skill. REVIEWED+AUDITED → REVISED transition (loops until ≥39/44 with zero critical flags across all active critics, or the iteration cap). ([rjwalters/anvil](https://github.com/rjwalters/anvil/blob/HEAD/anvil/skills/memoir/commands/memoir-revise.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
